TL;DR: 该论文展示了通过拓扑波导中巨原子的非局域耦合实现光子束缚态的可编程控制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统波导QED中的光子态工程通常基于局域光-物质相互作用，这限制了其对光子束缚态空间结构的控制能力。本文旨在探索通过结合拓扑能带结构和非局域巨原子耦合来实现对束缚光子态的更高级控制。

Method: 将巨原子耦合到Su-Schrieffer-Heeger拓扑波导中，利用拓扑能隙保护的可控能级交叉。通过绝热地扫描原子失谐穿过交叉点，实现不同光子束缚态之间的受控交换。在双巨原子配置中实现空间分裂态到组合态的转换，在三巨原子配置中实现形状保持的光子转移。

Result: 展示了拓扑能隙保护的可控能级交叉，实现了高保真度的空间分裂态到组合态的转换，以及通过连续带内交叉实现了稳健的形状保持光子转移。

Conclusion: 拓扑性和非局域光-物质耦合的结合为波导QED平台中束缚光子态的可编程控制提供了新机制，扩展了光子态工程的能力。

Abstract: Photonic state engineering in waveguide QED is typically based on local light-matter interactions. This limits its control over the spatial structure of bound photonic states. Here, we demonstrate a distinct mechanism arising from the interplay between nonlocal giant-atom coupling and topological band structure. Specifically, we consider giant atoms coupled to a Su-Schrieffer-Heeger waveguide and show that this configuration enables a controllable energy-level crossing protected by the topological gap. Adiabatically sweeping the atomic detuning across the crossing leads to a controlled exchange between distinct photonic bound states. In a two-giant-atom configuration, this mechanism achieves high-fidelity conversion of a spatially splitting state into a combining state. Extending this scheme to three-giant atoms, we further realize robust, shape-preserving photon transfer mediated by sequential in-gap crossings. Our results demonstrate how topology and nonlocal light-matter coupling can be combined to achieve programmable control of bound photonic states in waveguide QED platforms.

</details>


### [91] [Spectroscopic measurement of the Casimir-Polder force in the intermediate regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14721)
*K. Ton,G. Kestler,D. A. Steck,J. T. Barreiro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究首次直接测量了锶原子与介质表面在中间距离（约189nm）的卡西米尔-波尔德力，通过光谱探测原子能级的kHz频移，验证了QED理论计算


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卡西米尔-波尔德效应是量子真空涨落的重要表现，但在中间距离（原子-表面分离距离与原子主导跃迁波长相当）的实验观测一直依赖间接方法。需要直接测量该区域的CP力以验证理论预测

Method: 使用魔幻波长光学晶格将超冷锶原子限制在距介质表面189(2)nm处，通过光谱学方法探测CP效应引起的原子能级kHz频移，该距离与原子主导的461nm跃迁波长相当

Result: 测量结果与QED理论计算吻合良好，与短程近似不同，也排除了长程近似。首次在中间距离直接观测到CP力，频移为kHz量级

Conclusion: 该工作为研究不同表面性质和几何形状下的CP效应铺平了道路，有助于探索原子-表面势的张量性质，对开发混合原子光学-磁量子器件具有重要意义

Abstract: The Casimir-Polder (CP) effect -- the force between a neutral atom and an uncharged conducting plate in empty space -- is an intriguing consequence of quantum vacuum fluctuations. The typically attractive CP potential crosses over from a scaling of $z^{-3}$ at short separations to $z^{-4}$ at long distances, where retardation effects due to the finite speed of light become important. At intermediate distances, where the atom--surface separation is of the order of the wavelength of the dominant atomic transition, experiments have so far relied on indirect methods, such as diffraction or quantum reflection, to observe the CP effect. Here, we directly reveal the CP force between strontium atoms and a dielectric surface via the induced shifts in the atomic energy levels in the intermediate regime. We spectroscopically probe the CP-induced kHz-frequency shift of ultracold atoms confined by a magic-wavelength optical lattice at 189(2)~nm from the surface -- on the scale of the dominant 461-nm transition. Our measurements agree well with QED calculations and differ from the short-range approximation, while excluding the long-distance one. This paves the way for studying the CP effect across various surface properties and geometries, as well as exploring the tensor nature of the atom-surface potential -- all important for the development of hybrid atomic optical-magnetic quantum devices.

</details>


### [92] [Quantum Mpemba Effect in Non-Equilibrium Quantum Thermometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14740)
*Zi-Shen Li,Yuxiang Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 量子Mpemba效应（QMpE）与量子测温之间存在基本联系：在非平衡早期热化阶段，最优测温初始态极可能展现QMpE，且热化速度快于大多数初始态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然量子Mpemba效应已在多种框架中被广泛研究，但其在量子信息处理中的实际应用价值尚未充分探索。本研究旨在探究QMpE与量子测温之间的关系，特别是在非平衡早期热化阶段进行测量的场景。

Method: 在马尔可夫模型框架下，严格证明了在早期热化阶段进行测量的非平衡场景中，最优测温初始态极可能展现量子Mpemba效应。通过理论分析建立了QMpE与量子测温之间的数学联系。

Result: 研究结果表明：1）最优测温初始态极可能展现量子Mpemba效应；2）这些初始态的热化速度快于大多数初始态；3）QMpE与量子测温之间存在基本联系；4）QMpE可用于增强量子探针的温度估计能力。

Conclusion: 量子Mpemba效应与量子测温之间存在基本联系，QMpE可被利用来增强量子探针的温度估计能力。这一发现揭示了量子热力学与测温学之间的深层关系，为量子信息处理中的热管理应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: The quantum Mpemba effect (QMpE) describes an anomalous thermalization phenomenon in which quantum states initially far from equilibrium can approach thermal equilibrium faster than states that begin closer to it. While this effect has been extensively studied in various frameworks, its practical implications for quantum information processing remain largely unexplored. We investigate the relationship between QMpE and quantum thermometry, focusing on non-equilibrium scenarios where measurements are performed during early-stage thermalization. In a Markovian model, we rigorously prove that the initial states that are optimal for thermometry exhibit QMpE with high probability and thermalize faster than most initial states. Our results reveal a fundamental connection between quantum thermodynamics and thermometry, suggesting that QMpE can be harnessed to enhance temperature estimation with quantum probes.

</details>


### [93] [Activating entanglement and EPR steering from continuous-variable resources using witness-based measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14757)
*Kaustav Chatterjee,Ulrik Lund Andersen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于见证算子的连续变量资源量化框架，将CV资源操作性地激活为DV双粒子纠缠或EPR导引，建立了WN、GNG、SNG三种CV资源理论的无限族有界见证单调量，并构建了见证依赖的CPTP测量-制备信道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一种通用框架来量化连续变量资源并将其操作性地激活为离散变量纠缠或EPR导引，为实验可访问的DV相关性提供直接操作解释。

Method: 引入基于见证算子的框架，定义无限族有界见证单调量（通过见证算子的盒约束索引），对于闭凸自由集证明这些单调量具有忠实性、强单调性、Lipschitz连续性和凸性；对于闭非凸自由集，通过双拷贝提升实现忠实性；构建见证依赖的CPTP测量-制备信道，输出两量子比特Werner态。

Result: 对于n=m=1的代表性情况，在该见证依赖激活族内可获得的最优纠缠和EPR导引与基础单调量精确成正比；框架应用于奇宇称态、纯损耗单光子态和GKP态，推导了纯态GNG和SNG的显式下界。

Conclusion: 闭CV自由集允许基于见证的量化器，这些量化器在实验可访问的DV相关性方面具有直接操作解释，为连续变量资源理论提供了统一的激活和量化框架。

Abstract: We introduce a general witness-based framework for quantifying and operationally activating continuous-variable (CV) resources into discrete-variable (DV) bipartite entanglement or Einstein- Podolsky-Rosen (EPR) steering. For the three standard CV resource theories associated with Wigner negativity (WN), genuine non-Gaussianity (GNG), and standard non-Gaussianity (SNG), we define infinite families of bounded-witness monotones indexed by box constraints on the witness operators. For closed convex free sets, these monotones are faithful, strongly monotonic under free instruments, Lipschitz continuous, and convex. For closed nonconvex free sets, we show that faithfulness requires a two-copy lift and formulate the corresponding strong-monotonicity statement in the lifted theory. We further construct witness-dependent completely positive trace-preserving (CPTP) measure-and-prepare channels whose outputs are two-qubit Werner states. For the representative case n = m = 1, the optimal entanglement and EPR steering attainable within this witness-dependent activation family are exactly proportional to the underlying monotones. We illustrate the framework with odd-parity states, pure-loss single-photon states, and Gottesman- Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) states, and derive explicit lower bounds for pure-state GNG and SNG. More broadly, our results show that closed CV free sets admit witness-based quantifiers with a direct operational interpretation in terms of experimentally accessible DV correlations.

</details>


### [94] [Coherence dynamics in quantum algorithm for linear systems of equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14801)
*Linlin Ye,Zhaoqi Wu,Shao-Ming Fei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了HHL量子算法中演化态的相干性动力学，分析了相位估计算子和逆相位估计算子的相干性如何依赖于系统参数和成功概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子相干性是量子力学和量子信息处理的基本问题。研究HHL量子算法中演化态的相干性动力学，有助于理解量子算法执行过程中相干性的变化规律，为优化量子算法性能提供理论基础。

Method: 使用Tsallis相对α熵相干性和l_{1,p}范数相干性作为度量工具，分析HHL算法中相位估计算子P和逆相位估计算子~P的相干性。研究相干性如何依赖于|b⟩在A特征基下的分解系数β_i、A的特征值以及算法成功概率P_s。

Result: 相位估计算子P的相干性依赖于系数β_i；逆相位估计算子~P的相干性依赖于β_i、A的特征值和成功概率P_s，当α∈(1,2]时，~P的相干性随成功概率增加而减小；相干性的变化随成功概率增加而耗尽，并依赖于A的特征值和成功概率。

Conclusion: HHL量子算法中演化态的相干性动力学与算法参数密切相关，特别是成功概率对相干性变化有显著影响。这些发现有助于深入理解量子算法执行过程中量子资源的演化规律。

Abstract: Quantum coherence is a fundamental issue in quantum mechanics and quantum information processing. We explore the coherence dynamics of the evolved states in HHL quantum algorithm for solving the linear system of equation $A\overrightarrow{x}=\overrightarrow{b}$. By using the Tsallis relative $α$ entropy of coherence and the $l_{1,p}$ norm of coherence, we show that the operator coherence of the phase estimation $P$ relies on the coefficients $β_{i}$ obtained by decomposing $|b\rangle$ in the eigenbasis of $A$. We prove that the operator coherence of the inverse phase estimation $\widetilde{P}$ relies on the coefficients $β_{i}$, eigenvalues of $A$ and the success probability $P_{s}$, and it decreases with the increase of the probability when $α\in(1,2]$. Moreover, the variations of coherence deplete with the increase of the success probability and rely on the eigenvalues of $A$ as well as the success probability.

</details>


### [95] [Quantum Thermometry of External Phonon Reservoirs in Driven Open Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14855)
*Yildiz Berk Ates*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了相干驱动二能级量子系统在欧姆声子环境中量子费希尔信息（QFI）的非单调温度敏感性，发现中等耦合强度下存在最优热传感区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究环境相互作用（通常被视为有害退相干源）如何能被工程化为优化固态量子温度计性能的资源，探索超越标准弱耦合近似的声子诱导重整化效应。

Method: 采用幺正极化子变换处理相干驱动二能级量子系统与欧姆声子环境的耦合，考虑声子诱导重整化效应，分析量子费希尔信息（QFI）的温度敏感性。

Result: 量子费希尔信息在中等系统-环境耦合强度下出现显著峰值，表明存在最优热传感区域；热测量精度在超弱和强耦合极限下消失，但适当调谐的非平衡稳态能显著增强敏感性。

Conclusion: 环境相互作用可被工程化为优化固态量子温度计性能的资源，通过调控非平衡稳态和耦合强度，可在中等耦合区域实现显著增强的热传感能力。

Abstract: We investigate the non-monotonic temperature sensitivity of a coherently driven two-level quantum system coupled to an Ohmic phonon environment. By employing a unitary polaron transformation, we account for phonon-induced renormalization effects that go beyond the standard weak-coupling approximations. Our analysis reveals that the Quantum Fisher Information (QFI) exhibits a prominent peak at an intermediate system-environment coupling strength, identifying an optimal regime for thermal sensing. This behavior emerges from a fundamental competition between environment-induced dissipation enhancement and the exponential suppression of system parameters due to phonon dressing. We demonstrate that while thermometric precision vanishes in both the ultra-weak and strong coupling limits, a properly tuned nonequilibrium steady state can significantly enhance sensitivity. These results suggest that environmental interactions, often viewed as detrimental decoherence sources, can be engineered as a resource to optimize the performance of solid-state quantum thermometers.

</details>


### [96] [Split-Evolution Quantum Phase Estimation for Particle-Conserving Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14921)
*Megan Cerys Rowe,Carlo A. Gaggioli,Ludmila Szulakowska,David Muñoz Ramo,David Zsolt Manrique*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: SE-QPE是一种改进的量子相位估计算法，通过CSWAP干涉替代受控时间演化，降低资源消耗，在Quantinuum H2量子计算机上进行了硬件演示


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子相位估计（QPE）中的受控时间演化操作资源消耗大，特别是在处理粒子守恒哈密顿量时。SE-QPE旨在通过结构优化减少量子门数量和电路深度，提高实际量子硬件的执行效率

Method: SE-QPE将传统QPE中的受控时间演化替换为CSWAP门实现的干涉机制，在目标寄存器和参考寄存器之间进行交换操作。该方法保持相位寄存器输出分布不变，兼容非精确本征态，并允许两个寄存器并行演化

Result: 在Trotter化双因子化学哈密顿量中，SE-QPE在高相位幂次下优势明显。对于FeMoco活性空间，渐近减少约33%的CX门数量、25%的T门数量，CX层深度比为3/N。在Quantinuum H2-2上的四量子比特乙烯模型演示中，成功获得6相位比特的精确能量，辅助寄存器提供有效的错误检测

Conclusion: SE-QPE通过CSWAP干涉机制有效减少量子相位估计的资源需求，特别适用于高精度相位估计场景。硬件演示验证了其实际可行性，辅助寄存器可作为错误检测工具。结合传统QPE和SE-QPE的混合实现是优化量子化学模拟的有用策略

Abstract: We present a hardware demonstration and resource analysis of split-evolution quantum phase estimation (SE-QPE) on a Quantinuum System Model H2 quantum computer. SE-QPE is a modification to canonical QPE for particle-conserving Hamiltonians in which controlled time evolution is replaced by CSWAP-based interference between a target register and a reference register. For factorizations of time evolution with a shared eigenbasis, SE-QPE preserves the phase-register outcome distribution of canonical QPE and, unlike with compute--uncompute substitutions, it remains compatible with non-exact eigenstates. The substitution removes controlled-simulation overhead and enables parallel evolution on two registers, reducing the depth of each phase-kickback block. Resource analysis for Trotterized double-factorized chemistry Hamiltonians shows that the substitution becomes increasingly favorable at higher phase powers, as such combining QPE and SE-QPE implementations can be a useful option. Over a range of FeMoco active spaces, SE-QPE reduces time evolution resources, with asymptotic reductions of about 33% in CX count, 25% in $T$ count, and an asymptotic depth ratio of $3/N$ for CX layers. On Quantinuum H2-2, a four-qubit model ethylene demonstration with explicit inverse QFT and repeated phase-kickback steps up to 6 phase bits yields distinct energies and shows the auxiliary registers provide useful error detection filters.

</details>


### [97] [Learning to Concatenate Quantum Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14931)
*Nico Meyer,Christopher Mutschler,Dominik Seuß,Andreas Maier,Daniel D. Scherer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于学习的级联量子纠错码自适应选择方法，通过估计每级后的有效噪声通道并相应选择下一级码，显著减少实现目标逻辑错误率所需的量子比特数


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 级联量子纠错码虽然能通过双指数降低逻辑错误率，但噪声结构在级联过程中会发生变化，使得选择最优码序列变得困难。需要自动化方法来根据噪声结构变化自适应选择下一级纠错码

Method: 采用基于学习的方法：1) 估计每级纠错后的有效噪声通道；2) 当噪声具有足够结构时，使用学习定制的非加法编码器；3) 当噪声接近均匀时，切换为标准纠错码。形成级联自适应选择策略

Result: 仿真显示，这种级联自适应方法相比单独级联稳定子码，能以更少量子比特实现目标逻辑错误率。对于强结构噪声，量子比特数减少可达两个数量级

Conclusion: 这种混合的、基于学习的策略为早期容错量子计算提供了一个有前景的工具，能够根据噪声结构变化自适应优化纠错码选择，显著降低资源需求

Abstract: Concatenating quantum error correction codes scales error correction capability by driving logical error rates down double-exponentially across levels. However, the noise structure shifts under concatenation, making it hard to choose an optimal code sequence. We automate this choice by estimating the effective noise channel after each level and selecting the next code accordingly. In particular, we use learning-based methods to tailor small, non-additive encoders when the noise exhibits sufficient structure, then switch to standard codes once the noise is nearly uniform. In simulations, this level-wise adaptation achieves a target logical error rate with far fewer qubits than concatenating stabilizer codes alone--reducing qubit counts by up to two orders of magnitude for strongly structured noise. Therefore, this hybrid, learning-based strategy offers a promising tool for early fault-tolerant quantum computing.

</details>


### [98] [Introducing a novel $Z_{4n}$-detection scheme to enhance the performance of quantum LiDAR systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14935)
*Priyanka Sharma,Manoj K Mishra,Devendra Kumar Mishra*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种新的量子LiDAR检测方案Z_{4n}，仅当光子数为4n时才视为点击，相比传统Z检测方案显著提升了分辨率和相位灵敏度工作范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子LiDAR系统中，检测方案对分辨率和灵敏度至关重要。传统Z检测方案（任何光子数都视为点击）存在局限性，需要开发更优的检测方案来提升系统性能。

Method: 提出Z_{4n}检测方案，仅当光子数为4n（n为自然数）时才视为点击，其余情况视为无点击。采用四相干态叠加态（SFCS）和真空态作为输入，研究基于马赫-曾德尔干涉仪（MZI）的量子LiDAR系统性能。

Result: 相比传统Z检测方案，Z_{4n}检测方案在分辨率上获得显著提升，相位灵敏度的工作范围更宽，表现出更优的测量性能。

Conclusion: Z_{4n}检测方案为量子LiDAR传感技术提供了更准确、更灵敏的测量能力，有望推动该领域的技术进步。

Abstract: In a quantum LiDAR system, to achieve a better resolution and sensitivity, detection scheme plays an important role. We propose a novel detection scheme in which the photo detector considers only the $4n$ number of photons, where $n \in \mathbb{N}$, as a click and the rest of them as a no-click. Similar to the $Z$-detection scheme, where we get a click for any number of photons, we termed this measurement as $Z_{4n}$-detection scheme. By employing superposition of four coherent states (SFCS) and vacuum as input we investigate the performance of Mach-Zehnder interferometer (MZI) based quantum LiDAR systems. We found a significant enhancement in resolution and broader working point for the phase sensitivity in comparison to the $Z$-detection scheme. Our findings highlight the advantages of our approach and suggest promising advancements in the field of quantum LiDAR sensing technology, providing a pathway for more accurate and sensitive measurement capabilities.

</details>


### [99] [Floquet dynamical quantum phase transitions in periodically flux-quenched systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14946)
*Wen-Hui Nie,Mei-Yu Zhang,Lin-Cheng Wang,Chong Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了周期性驱动量子系统中的Floquet动力学量子相变，通过扩展XY自旋链模型和周期性通量淬灭协议，分析了微运动周期内通量差异对FDQPTs的影响，并推广了淬灭保真度概念到周期性驱动系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Floquet动力学量子相变揭示了周期性驱动量子系统中的非平衡临界现象，但其内在机制尚未完全理解。作者旨在探究周期性协议如何控制量子多体系统中的非平衡相变，特别是微运动周期内通量差异对FDQPTs的影响。

Method: 采用扩展XY自旋链模型，施加周期性通量淬灭协议。通过分析Loschmidt回波、速率函数、动力学拓扑序参数等物理量研究FDQPTs。推广淬灭保真度概念到周期性驱动系统，提出Floquet淬灭保真度，并讨论FDQPTs的充要条件。

Result: 发现FDQPTs的出现由Floquet保真度条件和分段持续时间共同决定，不同于传统的单次淬灭场景。微运动周期内的通量差异显著影响FDQPTs的出现。提出的框架可应用于任意周期性驱动参数。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解周期性协议如何控制量子多体系统中的非平衡相变提供了基础见解，建立了适用于任意周期性驱动参数的通用框架，深化了对Floquet动力学量子相变机制的理解。

Abstract: Floquet dynamical quantum phase transitions (FDQPTs) reveal many nonequilibrium critical phenomena in periodically driven quantum systems, and their underlying mechanisms have attracted deep attention in recent years. In this paper, we consider an extended XY spin chain under a periodic flux-quench protocol, and demonstrate the effect of the flux difference within each micromotion period on the emergence of FDQPTs, by analyzing physical quantities such as the Loschmidt echo, rate function, and dynamical topological order parameter (DTOP), etc. We also generalize the concept of quench fidelity to periodically driven systems, i.e., Floquet quench fidelity, and discuss the necessary and sufficient conditions for FDQPTs. In contrast to conventional single-quench scenarios, the occurrence of FDQPTs is determined by the requirement of Floquet fidelity condition and segment duration. Our framework may be applied generally to arbitrary periodically driven parameters, providing fundamental insights into how periodic protocols control nonequilibrium phase transitions in quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [100] [Quantum gravimetry with mechanical qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14950)
*Xiao-Wen Huo,Jun-Hong An,Peng-Bo Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于悬浮介观粒子的新型量子重力测量方案，通过直接利用机械量子比特作为重力传感器，避免了传统方案中辅助量子系统抵消质量优势的问题，实现了更高的灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于悬浮介观粒子的量子重力计未能充分利用粒子的大质量优势，因为其常用的辅助量子系统抵消了质量作为资源的作用。需要克服这一限制，开发能够直接利用机械量子比特作为重力传感器的新方案。

Method: 提出使用悬浮粒子形成的机械量子比特直接作为重力传感器，无需依赖辅助量子系统。该方法通过直接读取粒子在重力影响下的运动来实现重力测量。还将方案推广到使用机械猫态量子比特作为重力传感器。

Result: 灵敏度随质量m呈m^{-1/2}缩放。使用机械猫态量子比特时，灵敏度进一步随平均声子数N呈N^{-1/2}缩放。在实验可实现参数范围内，灵敏度可达0.1 μGal/√Hz，比传统方案提高两个数量级。同时达到质量和声子数的双重标准量子极限。

Conclusion: 该方案通过直接利用机械量子比特作为重力传感器，克服了传统方案的局限性，实现了质量和声子数的双重标准量子极限，为紧凑型高灵敏度量子重力测量提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Levitated mesoscopic particles hold the promise of revolutionizing gravity sensing by using quantum effects. However, conventional quantum gravimeters based on such systems fail to harness the intrinsic large-mass advantage of the particles, because their commonly utilized auxiliary quantum systems counteract the role of mass as a resource. To overcome this limitation, we propose a quantum gravimetry by directly using the mechanical qubit (QM) formed by a levitated particle as the gravity sensor. Without resorting to the auxiliary quantum system, our scheme enables a straightforward readout of the particle's motion under gravitational influence. The obtained sensitivity behaves as a $m^{-1/2}$-scaling with the mass $m$. We also generalize our scheme to the \textit{mechanical cat qubit} as the gravity sensor. The sensitivity further scales as $N^{-1/2}$ with the mean phonon number $N$. In the experimentally realizable parameter regime, a sensitivity on the order of $0.1~ \text{\textmu}\text{Gal}/\sqrt{\text{Hz}}$ can be achieved, which outperforms the traditional schemes by two orders of magnitude. Reaching the \textit{double standard quantum limits} with $m$ and $N$ simultaneously, our scheme provides a feasible route toward compact high-sensitivity quantum gravimetry.

</details>


### [101] [Three ways to share a QPU: Scheduling strategies for hybrid Quantum-HPC applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14955)
*Marco Cipollini,Simone Rizzo,Sergio Iserte,Paolo Viviani,Giacomo Vitali,Matteo Barbieri,Gabriella Bettonte,Elisabetta Boella,Fulvio Ganz,Roberto Rocco,Orazio Spina,Antonio J. Peña,Petter Sandås,Iacopo Colonnelli,Alberto Scionti,Chiara Vercellino,Emanuele Dri,Jonathan Frassineti,Sara Marzella,Andrea Muratori,Daniele Ottaviani,Olivier Terzo,Bartolomeo Montrucchio,Daniele Gregori*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了三种HPC-量子计算资源调度方法：基于时间的复用、动态资源管理和工作流分解，实验证明这些方法在不同工作负载场景下能有效优化混合量子-经典计算平台的资源利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子计算技术成熟，将其集成到现有高性能计算基础设施成为下一代计算系统的核心目标。然而，量子与经典编程模型不匹配、量子软件栈成熟度有限、量子处理单元稀缺等问题，需要超越标准HPC机制的调度策略来管理这种异构且受限的资源。

Method: 研究了三种HPC-QC资源调度方法：1) 基于时间的复用(time-based multiplexing)；2) 动态资源管理(dynamic resource management)；3) 工作流分解(workflow decomposition)。在生产的HPC集群和真实量子硬件上进行实验验证。

Result: 可塑性策略和工作流策略显著优化了经典资源利用率，分别减少消耗达45.7%和64%，最适合量子与经典工作负载均衡的混合作业。相反，时间复用增强了QPU利用率并减少了集群级执行时间，最适合经典-量子工作负载高度不平衡的场景。

Conclusion: 研究结果证明了为混合HPC-QC环境定制调度策略的实际可行性，并强调了它们在构建高效、可扩展的下一代量子加速设施软件栈中的互补性。

Abstract: As quantum computing (QC) technologies mature, their integration into established high-performance computing (HPC) infrastructures is becoming a central objective for next-generation computing systems. However, unlocking the potential of hybrid platforms for computationally demanding workloads remains challenging. The mismatch between quantum and classical programming models, the limited maturity of quantum software stacks, and the scarcity of quantum processing units (QPUs) above all, necessitate scheduling strategies that go beyond standard HPC mechanisms to manage such heterogeneous and constrained resources.
  To address this issue, we investigate three distinct methodologies for HPC-QC resource scheduling: time-based multiplexing, dynamic resource management, and workflow decomposition. Experimental validation on production HPC clusters and real quantum hardware demonstrates the effectiveness of these approaches under different workload scenarios. Malleability and workflow strategies significantly optimize classical resource utilization, reducing consumption by up to 45.7% and 64% respectively, proving to be best fitted for hybrid jobs where quantum and classical workloads are evenly balanced. Conversely, time-multiplexing enhances QPU utilization and reduces execution time at the cluster level, making it the optimal strategy for the opposite context, which is characterized by high classical-quantum workload imbalances. These findings underscore the practical viability of tailored scheduling strategies for hybrid HPC-QC environments and highlight their complementarity in building efficient, scalable software stacks for next-generation quantum-accelerated facilities.

</details>


### [102] [Ultrafast all-optical quantum teleportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14959)
*Takumi Suzuki,Takaya Hoshi,Akito Kawasaki,Shotaro Oki,Konhi Ichii,Hironari Nagayoshi,Kazuma Takahashi,Takahiro Kashiwazaki,Taichi Yamashima,Asuka Inoue,Takeshi Umeki,Tatsuki Sonoyama,Kan Takase,Warit Asavanant,Mamoru Endo,Akira Furusawa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究实现了1太赫兹带宽的全光量子隐形传态，突破了电子前馈约100兆赫兹的瓶颈，展示了光量子处理速度仅受非线性介质皮秒级响应限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光的固有载波频率可达数百太赫兹，理论上支持太赫兹时钟速率的信息处理。然而，在连续变量量子隐形传态中，电学前馈瓶颈将操作带宽限制在约100兆赫兹，无法充分利用光的极限速度。研究旨在突破这一电子限制，实现全光量子隐形传态。

Method: 采用全光量子隐形传态方法，通过光学方式传输贝尔测量结果，完全绕过电子前馈瓶颈。实验成功在太赫兹波段传输了真空态，并实时传输了42皮秒时间宽度的随机相干波包。

Result: 实现了1太赫兹带宽的全光量子隐形传态，宽带真空态的保真度达到F=0.784，动态相干波包的保真度为F=0.770。两个结果均严格超越了经典极限F=0.5，证明了超快速度下的真正量子隐形传态。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，光量子处理速度仅受非线性介质皮秒级响应时间的限制，而非经典电学接口。该方法为能够超越摩尔定律的太赫兹时钟量子计算机奠定了基础，并为高容量、电信兼容的量子互联网开辟了道路。

Abstract: Light's intrinsic carrier frequency of hundreds of terahertz theoretically enables information processing at terahertz clock rates. In optical quantum computing, continuous-variable quantum teleportation is the fundamental building block for deterministic logic operations. This protocol transfers unknown quantum states between nodes using quantum entanglement and real-time feedforward of measurement outcomes. However, electrical feedforward bottlenecks currently restrict operational bandwidths to approximately 100 megahertz, preventing the exploitation of light's ultimate speed. Here we show 1-terahertz-bandwidth all-optical quantum teleportation, completely bypassing this electronic limitation. By transferring Bell measurement outcomes optically, we successfully teleported vacuum states across the terahertz band and real-time random coherent wavepackets with a 42-picosecond temporal width. Evaluating the intrinsic state transfer quality, we achieved teleportation fidelities of $\mathcal{F}=0.784$ for the broadband vacuum states and $\mathcal{F}=0.770$ for the dynamic coherent wavepackets. Both results strictly surpass the classical limit of $\mathcal{F}=0.5$, demonstrating genuine quantum teleportation at ultrafast speeds. Our results establish that optical quantum processing speeds are constrained solely by the nonlinear medium's 1-picosecond-scale response, rather than classical electrical interfaces. This methodology provides a cornerstone for terahertz-clock quantum computers capable of overcoming Moore's law, and paves the way for a high-capacity, telecom-compatible quantum internet.

</details>


### [103] [Unconventional Photon Blockade in a Symmetrically Driven Nonlinear Dimer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14963)
*Hamid Ohadi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在对称Kerr二聚体中，通过90°相位差的等幅驱动场实现非常规光子阻塞，无需强非线性即可产生强反聚束光


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统光子阻塞需要强非线性（U >> γ），而非常规光子阻塞通过量子干涉效应在弱非线性条件下实现光子反聚束。本文旨在开发一种在对称系统中实现稳定、可观测的非常规光子阻塞方案

Method: 采用对称Kerr二聚体（两个耦合腔），施加90°相位差的等幅驱动场。通过量子干涉破坏双光子激发路径，实现光子阻塞。最小腔间耦合J_min = γ/4，非线性U << γ。通过调节驱动相位补偿制造缺陷

Result: 实现了强反聚束光发射（g²(0) << 1），二阶关联函数平滑无振荡，可直接用标准探测器分辨。方案在连续波和脉冲激发下均有效，制造缺陷可通过重新调谐驱动相位完全补偿

Conclusion: 该方案在标准光子分子中可实现，无需后制造腔修整，为弱非线性条件下产生单光子源提供了实用方案

Abstract: We demonstrate unconventional photon blockade in a symmetric Kerr dimer driven with equal-amplitude fields at a $90^\circ$ phase difference. The minimum inter-cavity coupling is $J_{\min} = γ/4$ at a Kerr nonlinearity $U \ll γ$ achievable in standard photonic molecules. The quadrature-driven site emits strongly antibunched light with a smooth, oscillation-free second-order correlator directly resolvable with standard detectors. The scheme operates under continuous-wave and pulsed excitation, and fabrication disorder can be fully compensated by re-tuning the drive phase, removing the need for post-fabrication cavity trimming.

</details>


### [104] [Recurrence Time for Finite Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14995)
*Chaitanya Gupta,Anthony J. Short*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了有限量子系统中所有状态同时回归到初始配置所需的时间，定义了量子系统的"回归时间"，并利用狄利克雷逼近定理给出了连续时间和离散时间下的界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限量子系统中所有状态同时回归到初始配置的时间特性，这对于理解量子系统的动力学行为和周期性具有重要意义。量子系统的回归现象是量子力学中的一个基本问题，但所有状态同时回归的时间界限尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 定义了量子系统的"回归时间"概念，即所有时间演化状态都接近其初始配置，且至少有一个状态在此期间显著偏离的时间点。利用狄利克雷逼近定理，通过将实数差用有理数逼近的问题与回归时间界限联系起来，建立了连续时间和离散时间下的数学框架。

Result: 获得了量子系统回归时间的界限，通过将回归时间问题转化为实数差的有理逼近问题，并提出了相关的数学结果，从而得到了更紧的回归时间界限。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了有限量子系统回归时间的理论框架，通过数学方法获得了回归时间的界限，深化了对量子系统动力学行为和周期性的理解，为量子信息处理和量子计算中的时间控制问题提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study the time it takes for all states of a finite quantum system to return simultaneously to their original configuration. In particular, we define the recurrence time for a quantum system to be the time at which all time-evolved states are close to their initial configuration, and at least one state has deviated significantly during this interval. Considering finite-dimensional quantum systems evolving unitarily, we find bounds on this notion of recurrence time, for continuous time and discrete time, by using Dirichlet's approximation theorem. We show how the problem of finding a bound on recurrence time can be related to approximating the difference of real numbers by rationals. We present a mathematical result on the latter, which we then use to obtain tighter bounds on recurrence time.

</details>


### [105] [Investigating Spectral Dynamics and Spin Signatures of a Mechanically Isolated Quantum Emitter in hBN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14998)
*Sajedeh Shahbazi,Alexander Pachl,Kathrin Schwer,Patrick Maier,Alexander Kubanek*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了六方氮化硼中机械隔离量子发射器的光谱不稳定性和自旋相关性质，通过集成到共面波导实现了高亮度共振荧光，并阐明了电荷驱动光谱涨落与自旋相关暗态共同影响光学循环动力学的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 六方氮化硼中的机械隔离缺陷中心是有前景的相干量子发射器，但其光谱不稳定性持续存在，且自旋相关性质仍不清楚。需要深入理解这些缺陷的光学循环动力学机制。

Method: 将单个机械隔离的量子发射器集成到共面波导上，进行高分辨率光谱学、时间分辨光谱学、磁场依赖光致发光、光学检测磁共振和泵浦-探测测量。

Result: 发射器表现出异常明亮的共振荧光，饱和计数率超过10 Mc/s；发现两个紧密间隔的零声子线跃迁，具有不同的光谱扩散动力学；蓝光照明可重新分配两个跃迁间的发射；观察到毫秒尺度弛豫动力学和磁场依赖荧光对比度，揭示了亚稳态暗态中的自旋依赖布居动力学。

Conclusion: 研究阐明了电荷驱动光谱涨落和自旋相关暗态如何共同塑造光学循环动力学，为理解六方氮化硼中量子发射器的光谱不稳定性和自旋性质提供了关键见解。

Abstract: Mechanically isolated defect centers in hexagonal boron nitride are promising coherent quantum emitters, yet spectral instabilities persist, and their spin-related nature remains unclear. Here we investigate a single mechanically isolated quantum emitter in hBN integrated onto a coplanar waveguide. The emitter exhibits exceptionally bright resonant fluorescence with saturation count rates exceeding $10\,\mathrm{Mc/s}$. High-resolution spectroscopy reveals two closely spaced zero-phonon-line transitions originating from the same defect complex. Time-resolved spectroscopy shows that these transitions exhibit markedly different spectral diffusion dynamics, consistent with distinct donor-acceptor-pair-like recombination pathways with different sensitivities to local electrostatic fluctuations. Off-resonant blue illumination redistributes emission between the two transitions and increases the emission duty cycle without significantly modifying the dominant spectral diffusion rates at low temperature, indicating repumping from long-lived shelving states. Magnetic-field-dependent photoluminescence, optically detected magnetic resonance, and pump-probe measurements reveal millisecond-scale relaxation dynamics and magnetic-field-dependent fluorescence contrast, demonstrating spin-dependent population dynamics in the metastable shelving state. These results clarify how charge-driven spectral fluctuations and spin-dependent shelving jointly shape the optical cycling dynamics.

</details>


### [106] [Runtime-efficient zero-noise extrapolation from mixed physical and logical data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15014)
*D. V. Babukhin,W. V. Pogosov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 混合量子纠错与误差缓解策略：结合少量纠错数据点与大量未纠错数据点进行零噪声外推，显著降低物理运行时间要求


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在容错前阶段，部分量子纠错与量子误差缓解预计将共存，但两者结合的资源优势尚未得到充分量化。需要研究混合数据集策略以降低资源需求。

Method: 研究基于混合数据集的零噪声外推法：少量低噪声纠错数据点（逻辑点）作为外推锚点，大量高噪声未纠错数据点（物理点）以较低运行成本扩大噪声基线。在纠错将有效门错误率从p抑制到γp的简单模型下，推导零噪声估计器的方差并比较达到目标精度所需的物理运行时间。

Result: 对于Richardson外推，混合数据策略减少了方差放大，当γ≤0.1时可将所需物理运行时间降低数个数量级。在六自旋横场伊辛模型的数字量子模拟中，混合物理/逻辑数据集产生更低方差的零噪声估计，在研究的参数范围内优于仅基于纠错数据的外推。

Conclusion: 混合纠错与误差缓解是容错前实现资源高效量子计算的实用途径，为预容错量子计算提供了有前景的策略。

Abstract: Partial quantum error correction and quantum error mitigation are expected to coexist in the pre-fault-tolerant regime, yet the resource advantage of combining them remains insufficiently quantified. We study zero-noise extrapolation constructed from mixed datasets that contain a small number of error-corrected data points together with data obtained without error correction. The low-noise logical points anchor the extrapolation, while the higher-noise physical points enlarge the noise baseline at a much smaller runtime cost. Under a simple model in which error correction suppresses the effective gate error rate from p to $γ$p, we derive the variance of the zero-noise estimator and compare the physical runtime required to reach a target precision. For Richardson extrapolation, the mixed-data strategy reduces variance amplification and can lower the required physical runtime by several orders of magnitude when $γ\leq 0.1$. As a proof of principle, we apply the method to digital quantum simulation of a six-spin transverse-field Ising model and find that mixed physical/logical datasets yield lower-variance zero-noise estimates and outperform extrapolation based only on error-corrected data in the parameter regime studied here. These results identify hybrid error correction and error mitigation as a practical route to resource-efficient quantum computation before full fault tolerance.

</details>


### [107] [Hidden Quantum Advantage near the Decoding Threshold of Decoded Quantum Interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15025)
*Maoxin Gao,Yan Chang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文揭示了现有量子优势边界分析的系统性低估问题，通过利用Perron特征向量集中性改进边界，在LDPC基准实例上发现了26个连续参数点存在量子优势但被原分析遗漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子干涉测量解码（DQI）量子优势区域边界分析（Jordan等人的定理10.1）系统性地低估了量子优势的实际范围，需要在标准基准实例上建立更准确的边界。

Method: 利用DQI三对角矩阵的谱结构，特别是Perron特征向量的集中性，用加权平均解码失败率ε̄ = Σ_k ε_k w_k²替代原分析中的最坏情况惩罚ε = max_k ε_k，建立了适用于任意有限域F_q的统一下界（主定理）。

Result: 在标准部分获胜LDPC基准实例上，发现了26个连续参数点（l∈[642,667]）存在量子优势（近似比达0.66），但原分析错误地宣称无量子优势（<s>/m < 0.5）。新边界严格改进了原边界。

Conclusion: 通过利用DQI的谱结构特征，建立了更准确的量子优势边界分析框架，揭示了量子优势区域比先前认知更广泛，为量子干涉测量解码的实际应用提供了更可靠的理论基础。

Abstract: Where is the true boundary of the quantum advantage region of decoded quantum interferometry (DQI)? The best existing answer is provided by Theorem 10.1 of Jordan et al., yet we show that this answer systematically underestimates the extent of quantum advantage. On the standard partial-win LDPC benchmark instance, there exist 26 consecutive parameter points (l in [642, 667]) at which Jordan's analysis declares no quantum advantage (<s>/m < 0.5), while quantum advantage is in fact present with an approximation ratio reaching 0.66. The root cause is that Jordan's bound penalizes the entire system with the worst-case Hamming-layer decoding failure rate epsilon = max_k epsilon_k, discarding the spectral structure of the DQI tridiagonal matrix. Exploiting the concentration of the Perron eigenvector, we replace the uniform penalty with the weighted average epsilon_bar = sum_k epsilon_k w_k^2 and establish a unified lower bound (Master Theorem) valid over arbitrary finite fields F_q, proving that it strictly improves upon the original bound from three independent sources.

</details>


### [108] [A NISQ-friendly Coined Quantum Walk Algorithm for Chaos-based Cryptographic Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15030)
*Natalie Gibson,Niklas Keckman,Andrea Marchesin,Matti Raasakka,Ilkka Tittonen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种新型懒散交替量子行走（LAQW）算法，其电路深度为O(n²+nt)，相比现有受控交替量子行走（CAQW）的O(n²t)有显著降低，适用于NISQ设备。将该算法应用于混沌对称密钥生成方案，作为量子熵源生成可复现的随机比特串序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有受控交替量子行走（CAQW）模型的电路深度为O(n²t)，在NISQ设备上实现困难。需要开发电路深度更低的量子行走算法，使其能够在当前噪声中等规模量子设备上实际应用，特别是在密码学领域。

Method: 提出懒散交替量子行走（LAQW）算法，通过优化电路结构将深度降低到O(n²+nt)。将该算法用作量子熵源，基于其底层概率分布和后续后处理方法生成可复现的随机比特串序列，构建混沌对称密钥生成方案。

Result: LAQW算法相比CAQW模型实现了显著的电路深度降低（从O(n²t)到O(n²+nt)）。在IBM FakeTorino后端模拟的量子噪声环境下，成功演示了128位密钥的可复现性。与CAQW在图像加密和哈希函数中的应用相比，LAQW在密码学应用中显示出更大潜力。

Conclusion: LAQW算法通过显著降低电路深度，使量子行走算法在NISQ设备上变得可行。该算法作为量子熵源在混沌对称密钥生成中的应用表明，LAQW模型在密码学领域具有实际应用价值和潜力。

Abstract: We present a novel lackadaisical alternating quantum walk (LAQW) algorithm whose circuit depth scales as $\mathcal{O}(n^2+nt)$ for a $n\times n$ lattice over $t$ time steps. We show that this is a significant depth reduction compared to the existing controlled alternating quantum walk (CAQW) model, which has a circuit depth that scales as $\mathcal{O}(n^2t)$ (Li et al., 2017, arXiv:1707.07389). This makes the implementation of the LAQW viable for Noisy Intermediate-scale Quantum (NISQ) devices. We then showcase the applicability of the LAQW algorithm by proposing a chaos-based symmetric-key generation scheme. Our approach uses the LAQW as a quantum entropy source from which reproducible random bitstring sequences are generated using the underlying probability distribution and subsequent post-processing methods. We provide a comprehensive evaluation of the LAQW algorithm and demonstrate the reproducibility of 128-bit keys under simulated quantum noise provided by IBM's FakeTorino backend. A direct comparison with the CAQW model, which has been used in image encryption and hash function schemes (Li et al., 2017, arXiv:1707.07389; Abd EL-Latif et al., 2020, ScienceDirect; Abd El-Latif, Abd El-Atty, and Venegas-Andraca, 2020, ScienceDirect), highlights the potential and usefulness of the LAQW model in cryptographic applications.

</details>


### [109] [SyQMA: A memory-efficient, symbolic and exact universal simulator for quantum error correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15043)
*George Umbrarescu,David Amaro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: SyQMA是一个用于模拟通用量子电路的符号量子模拟器，特别适用于量子纠错，能够计算电路参数的精确符号函数，支持动态量子程序采样，并执行电路级最大似然解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算的经典模拟在基础理论和实际应用中都至关重要。现有的模拟器在处理量子纠错、噪声建模和动态量子程序方面存在局限性，需要一种能够提供精确符号分析、支持噪声参数化并适用于量子纠错协议验证的模拟工具。

Method: 通过稳定子模拟器的直观扩展，将每个非Clifford Pauli旋转和非相干Pauli信道通过辅助量子比特和修改的迹进行紧凑表示。状态表示仅需多项式内存和时间，而计算期望值和测量概率在非Clifford旋转和确定性测量数量上呈指数时间但仅需多项式内存。

Result: SyQMA能够模拟带非相干Pauli噪声的通用量子电路，计算电路参数（旋转角度、测量结果、噪声率）的精确符号函数，支持动态量子程序采样，执行电路级最大似然解码，提供逻辑错误率的精确符号表达式，并验证容错稳定子和魔法态制备协议的故障距离。

Conclusion: SyQMA是一个功能强大的符号量子模拟器，特别适用于量子纠错应用，能够在不使用近似的情况下分析容错稳定子和魔法态制备，包括魔法态培养的第一阶段。该工具公开可用且开源，为量子计算模拟提供了重要的分析能力。

Abstract: The classical simulation of universal quantum circuits is crucial both fundamentally and practically for quantum computation. We propose SyQMA, a simulator with several convenient features, particularly suited for quantum error correction (QEC). SyQMA simulates universal quantum circuits with incoherent Pauli noise and computes exact expectation values and measurement probabilities as symbolic functions of circuit parameters: rotation angles, measurement outcomes, and noise rates. This simulator can sample measurement outcomes, enabling the simulation of dynamic quantum programs where circuit composition depends on prior measurement outputs. For QEC, it performs circuit-level maximum-likelihood decoding, provides exact symbolic expressions for logical error rates, and verifies the fault distance of fault-tolerant (FT) stabiliser and magic state preparation protocols. These features are enabled by an intuitive extension of stabiliser simulators, where each non-Clifford Pauli rotation and incoherent Pauli channel is compactly represented via auxiliary qubits and a modified trace. Representing the state requires only polynomial memory and time, while computing expectation values and measurement probabilities takes exponential time in the number of non-Clifford rotations and deterministic measurements, but only polynomial memory. The FT preparation of stabiliser and magic states, including the first stage of magic state cultivation, is analysed without approximations. We also exactly convert the disjoint error probabilities of a general multi-qubit Pauli channel to independent ones, a key step for creating and sampling from detector error models. The code is publicly available and open-source.

</details>


### [110] [Hardware Validation of DAGI via a Modular "Ridge" Signature and High-Order Synergistic Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15051)
*Petr Sramek*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在IBM量子硬件上验证DAGI框架，通过模运算约束实验展示硬件噪声下仍可检测高阶协同信息结构


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证DAGI框架在真实量子硬件上的可行性，特别是检测和量化与全局代数约束相关的高阶协同信息结构，这些结构在低阶边际分布中不可见

Method: 使用IBM Quantum硬件（ibm_torino）进行实验，设计模运算约束：两个4比特寄存器(u,v)满足v ≡ k·u (mod 16)。采用Sampler V2作业（8个密钥，每个1024次测量，共8192次）。通过Möbius反演管道计算信息分解，评估目标正协同信息CPS_K

Result: 硬件噪声下脊线仍存在：脊线命中概率0.1830（基线1/16），对比度2.93倍。密钥恢复优于随机：每测量准确率0.1689（随机0.125），每组字典恢复0.375。检测到显著的三阶目标正协同信息CPS_K=0.08788（p=0.004975），支持高阶协同信息主导的假设

Conclusion: DAGI框架能够在真实量子硬件噪声环境下检测和量化与已知全局代数约束相关的非平凡、硬件弹性的高阶信息结构，验证了其主要假设：可恢复的密钥信息主要是高阶/协同的，而非在低阶边际分布中可见

Abstract: We report a hardware validation of the DAGI (Directed Acyclic Graph Information) framework on IBM Quantum hardware using a small, controlled experiment whose ideal output distribution is constrained to a low-dimensional modular manifold (a "ridge"). For two $n$-bit registers $(u,v)$ with $n=4$ (modulus 16), each key instance $k$ induces an ideal relation $v \equiv k \cdot u \pmod{16}$, producing a visually distinct ridge in the joint $(u, v)$ distribution. Executed on ibm\_torino in a single Sampler V2 job (8 keys, 1024 shots/key, $N=8192$ total shots), the ridge persists under hardware noise with ridge-hit probability $p_{hit} = 0.1830$ (uniform baseline $1/16$), corresponding to a ridge contrast of $2.93\times$ (95\% bootstrap CI [2.80, 3.06]). Key recovery exceeds chance: per-shot accuracy 0.1689 (chance 0.125, 95\% Wilson CI [0.1610, 0.1772]), and per-group dictionary recovery 0.375 (chance 0.125). To test the central DAGI hypothesis -- that recoverable key information is predominantly high-order/synergistic rather than visible in low-order marginals -- we compute a Möbius-based information decomposition of $I(K;D_S)$ over detector-bit subsets $S$ via a Möbius inversion pipeline and evaluate targeted positive synergy $CPS_K$ at order $k_{max}=3$. We observe $CPS_K(k=3) = 0.08788$ with significance under label-shuffle permutation tests (accuracy $p=0.001996$, $CPS_K$ $p=0.004975$). Uniformity diagnostics show near-uniform single-bit marginals while correlation concentrates in specific low-order pairs, and a bootstrap reliability sweep confirms order-3 targeted synergy remains statistically reliable at the full 1024-shot target budget. These results support the claim that DAGI detects and quantifies nontrivial, hardware-resilient, higher-order information structure associated with a known global algebraic constraint.

</details>


### [111] [QLLVM: A Scalable Quantum-Classical Co-Compilation Framework based on LLVM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15094)
*Yu Zhu,Qiming Du,Yuqiong Jin,Woji He,Hang Lian,Xin Zhou,Jinchen Xu,Zheng Shan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: QLLVM是一个基于LLVM的量子-经典协同编译框架，提供端到端的编译工作流，将经典高性能程序（CUDA、MPI、C++）与量子程序统一编译为单一可执行文件，在NISQ时代提供高性能、可扩展的量子编译器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 满足NISQ时代对高性能、可扩展量子编译器的迫切需求，推动经典与量子计算的融合，为未来量子软件开发提供工业级编译基础设施。

Method: 采用三层设计：在MLIR Quantum方言中实现高级优化，然后降级到基于LLVM IR的QIR表示，进行低级优化和硬件映射。框架具有可扩展架构，与经典高性能计算无缝互操作。

Result: 在MQTBench基准测试套件上，QLLVM相比最先进的编译器减少了电路深度和门数量，在编译混合经典-量子程序方面显示出明显优势。

Conclusion: QLLVM提供了一个高效、灵活、工业级的量子-经典协同编译框架，为未来量子软件开发提供了强大的编译基础设施，在NISQ时代具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: To address the urgent need in the NISQ era for high-performance, scalable quantum compilers and to advance the integration of classical and quantum computing, we present QLLVM, an advanced Quantum-Classical co-compilation framework built on LLVM. To our knowledge, QLLVM delivers an end-to-end, LLVM-based compilation workflow that unifies the build of classical high-performance programs, including CUDA, MPI, and C++, together with quantum programs into a single executable. For quantum program compilation, QLLVM adopts a three-stage design: high-level optimizations are implemented in the MLIR Quantum dialect and then lowered to QIR, an LLVM IR-based representation, for low-level optimization and hardware mapping. Its extensible architecture and seamless interoperability with classical high-performance computing provide an efficient, flexible, industrial-grade compilation infrastructure for future quantum software development. Experimental results show that, on the MQTBench benchmark suite, QLLVM reduces circuit depth and gate counts compared with state-of-the-art compilers and demonstrates clear advantages in compiling hybrid classical-quantum programs.

</details>


### [112] [O3LS: Optimizing Lattice Surgery via Automatic Layout Searching and Loose Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15099)
*Chenghong Zhu,Xian Wu,Jiahan Chen,Keming He,Junjie Wu,Xin Wang,Lingling Lao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: O3LS是一个通过自动布局搜索和宽松调度优化晶格手术的框架，能同时减少空间和时间开销，降低逻辑错误率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于表面码的晶格手术是实现容错量子计算的有前途技术，但现有方法主要关注时间开销优化，忽略了旋转瓶颈和移动距离之间的权衡，导致资源利用效率低下，无法进一步降低量子计算失败率

Method: 提出O3LS框架，通过自动生成压缩数据布局减少空间需求，结合宽松调度算法和电路合成技术减少时间开销，实现空间和时间开销的平衡优化

Result: 相比标准布局减少28.0%空间开销，相比稀疏布局减少46.7%空间开销且不增加时间步数；在紧凑和标准数据布局设计中分别实现36.07%和24.76%的时间开销减少；逻辑错误率相比大型数据布局设计降低16%，相比主要关注并行化的编译器降低一个数量级

Conclusion: O3LS通过自动布局搜索和宽松调度实现了晶格手术的空间和时间开销平衡优化，有效降低了逻辑错误率，为大规模实用化量子计算提供了更高效的容错量子计算实现方案

Abstract: Toward the large-scale, practical realization of quantum computing, quantum error correction is essential. Among various quantum error-correcting codes, the surface code stands out as a leading candidate, and lattice surgery based on surface codes has emerged as a promising technique for fault-tolerant quantum computation (FTQC). However, implementing quantum algorithms using lattice surgery introduces both resource and time overhead. Existing approaches typically focus on large layout designs, with compiler passes aimed primarily at optimizing time overhead. This often overlooks the trade-off between rotation bottlenecks and movement distance, which leads to inefficient resource utilization and prevents further reduction of the quantum computation failure rate.
  To address these challenges, we introduce O3LS, a framework for optimizing lattice surgery through automatic layout search and loose scheduling. O3LS achieves an optimal balance by automatically generating squeezed data layouts to reduce space requirements and employing loose scheduling algorithms combined with circuit synthesis techniques to reduce time overhead, thereby effectively minimizing overall logical error rates. Numerical results indicate that O3LS can reduce space overhead by 28.0% over standard layouts and 46.7% over sparse layouts without increasing the number of time steps, leading to suppression of logical error rates by up to 16% relative to larger data layout designs. O3LS can also achieve time overhead reductions of 36.07% and 24.76% in compact and standard data layout designs, respectively. It suppresses logical error rates by up to an order of magnitude compared to prior compilers that focus primarily on maximizing parallelism.

</details>


### [113] [Low-rank geometry of two-qubit gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15102)
*Llorenç Balada Gaggioli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 基于两量子比特门的行列式几何框架，将门合成解释为到行列式簇的距离问题，为Weyl室提供操作几何并量化非局域复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为量子门合成提供几何框架，将门合成问题转化为到行列式簇的距离优化问题，从而量化量子门的非局域复杂度。

Method: 结合Weyl室表示与算子Schmidt理论，将门合成解释为到行列式簇的距离问题，使用三种不同的行列式代价函数形成合成适应的坐标系。

Result: 证明平方根iSWAP门是到局部操作簇最近的完美纠缠门，且没有完美纠缠门能被局部门以高于79.8%的平均门保真度近似；三种行列式代价函数编码非局域复杂度并重构Weyl室。

Conclusion: 基于行列式几何的框架为量子门合成提供了操作几何视角，将非局域复杂度量化为到行列式簇的距离，为量子计算中的门优化提供了新工具。

Abstract: We present a framework based on the determinantal geometry of two-qubit gates. Combining the Weyl chamber representation with operator Schmidt theory, we interpret gate synthesis as a distance problem to determinantal varieties. This gives an operational geometry to the Weyl chamber, quantifying nonlocal complexity. We show that the square root iSWAP gate is the closest perfect entangler to the variety of local operations, and that no perfect entangler can be approximated by a local gate with average gate fidelity above 79.8%. The three different determinantal costs form a synthesis-adapted coordinate system that encodes nonlocal complexity and generally reconstructs the Weyl chamber.

</details>


### [114] [General Static Solutions of the SU(2) Yang-Mills Equations from a Spin Vector Potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15110)
*Yu-Xuan Zhang,Jing-Ling Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了无源SU(2)杨-米尔斯方程的静态解，其中规范势显式依赖于自旋算符。通过向量势提取方法，推导了最一般的自旋向量势形式，并对静态解进行了完整分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究SU(2)杨-米尔斯方程中规范势显式依赖于自旋算符的静态解，旨在获得更一般的解分类，为微扰研究和自旋自由度与非阿贝尔规范场耦合的模型提供新的静态构型。

Method: 采用向量势提取方法，要求总角动量算符满足标准角动量代数，推导出最一般的自旋向量势形式。通过将静态ansatz代入杨-米尔斯方程并施加VPEA的角动量约束，得到一致性方程并求解。

Result: 获得了包含三个常数和两个径向函数的静态ansatz，通过求解一致性方程完成了静态解的完整分类，包括实数和复数解族。已知的简单SU(2)静态解作为特例被恢复，并发现了新的静态构型。

Conclusion: 该研究系统分类了SU(2)杨-米尔斯方程的静态解，为微扰研究和自旋自由度与非阿贝尔规范场耦合的模型提供了有价值的静态构型，扩展了对非阿贝尔规范场静态解的理解。

Abstract: We present a systematic study of static solutions to the source-free SU(2) Yang-Mills equations, in which the gauge potential explicitly depends on spin operators. By employing the \emph{vector potential extraction approach} (VPEA) -- which requires the total angular momentum operator (orbital plus spin) to satisfy the standard angular momentum algebra -- we derive the most general form of the spin vector potential. This leads to the static ansatz $\{ \vec{A} = [k_1(\hat{r}\times\vecΓ) + k_2\vecΓ + k_3(\vecΓ\cdot\hat{r})\hat{r}]/r, \varphi = f_1(r)\,(\vecΓ\cdot\hat{r}) + f_2(r)\}$, parametrized by three constants $\{k_1, k_2, k_3\}$ and two radial functions $\{f_1(r), f_2(r)\}$. Substituting this ansatz into the Yang-Mills equations and imposing the angular momentum constraints from the VPEA yields a set of consistency equations. Solving these equations provides a complete classification of static solutions, including both real and complex families. Known simple SU(2) static solutions are recovered as special cases. Our classification reveals new static configurations that could be valuable for non-perturbative studies and for models where spin degrees of freedom couple to non-Abelian gauge fields.

</details>


### [115] [Constraints on phantom codes from automorphism group bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15111)
*Arthur S. Morris,Daniel Malz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 幻象码（phantom codes）虽然能通过物理置换实现所有块内逻辑CNOT电路，但编码率受到对数上界的限制，k ≤ log₂(n+1)（k≠4时）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究幻象码的编码率限制，探索在实现高效逻辑CNOT电路的同时，编码能力能达到的理论上限。

Method: 通过数学证明建立幻象码的编码率上界，构造k=4的反例，分析CSS幻象码的饱和情况，并扩展到子系统码和局部酉门的情况。

Result: 证明二进制幻象码满足k ≤ log₂(n+1)（k≠4），构造了违反此界的(8,16,2)非稳定子幻象码，发现CSS幻象码中存在唯一饱和此界的码族。

Conclusion: 幻象码的"幻象性"（通过物理置换实现逻辑CNOT）以低编码率为代价，这一限制无法通过局部酉门或子系统码规避，揭示了量子码长度与其自同构群结构的基本关系。

Abstract: Executing a logical quantum circuit fault-tolerantly incurs a large spacetime overhead. Recent work has proposed and investigated phantom codes, defined by the property that every in-block logical $\mathrm{CNOT}$ circuit can be implemented with a physical permutation, a property that has the potential to greatly reduce the depth of compiled circuits. Here we show that phantomness comes at the cost of low encoding rate. Specifically, we prove that any binary phantom code encoding $k$ logical qubits into $n$ physical qubits with distance $d\geq 2$ obeys the bound $k\leq \log_2(n+1)$ for all $k\neq 4$. For $k=4$ we explicitly construct a nonstabiliser $(\!(8, 2^4, 2)\!)$ phantom code that violates the bound and has a transversal non-Clifford gate. We further show that, within the class of nontrivial CSS phantom codes with $k\neq 4$, there is a unique family of codes saturating this bound. In addition, we prove that this logarithmic ceiling cannot be circumvented by permitting additional local unitary gates, or by making use of subsystem codes: any subspace or subsystem code admitting a $\mathrm{SWAP}$-transversal implementation of every logical $\mathrm{CNOT}$ circuit is constrained to satisfy the same bound. These bounds follow from a general theorem relating the length of a quantum code to the structure of its automorphism group, a result which may find applications beyond phantom codes.

</details>


### [116] [Coherent control of optomechanical entanglement and steering via dual parametric amplification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15162)
*Jinhao Jia,Yingru Li,Ran Liang,Mei Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于相干控制的腔光力学系统量子关联工程方案，通过光学和力学参量放大与相干反馈回路联合调制，增强量子关联并提高其热噪声鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腔光力学系统在量子信息处理中具有重要应用，但量子关联通常对热噪声敏感且难以调控。需要开发能够增强量子关联强度、提高热噪声鲁棒性并实现高度可调谐的控制方案。

Method: 1. 构建包含非线性介质和薄膜的腔光力学系统；2. 通过泵浦实现光学和力学参量放大，调制频率和泵浦幅度可控；3. 引入相干反馈回路；4. 联合调制两种参量放大和反馈回路，工程化有效腔衰减率和量子涨落分布。

Result: 该方案能够显著增强量子关联强度，提高其对热噪声的鲁棒性，实现高度可调谐的强量子关联，为保护脆弱量子资源提供了有效途径。

Conclusion: 提出的相干控制方案为腔光力学系统实现高度可调谐、强且热噪声鲁棒的量子关联提供了有效途径，在保护脆弱量子资源方面具有重要应用前景。

Abstract: We propose a coherent-control scheme for engineering quantum correlations in a cavity optomechanical (COM) system consisting of a driven optical cavity with an embedded nonlinear medium and a membrane, assisted by a coherent feedback loop. The nonlinear medium and the membrane are pumped to implement optical and mechanical parametric amplifications with controllable modulation frequencies and pump amplitudes. Through the combined modulation of the two parametric amplifications and the coherent feedback loop, we engineer the effective cavity decay rate and the distribution of quantum fluctuations, thereby strengthening quantum correlations and improving their robustness against thermal noise. Our scheme provides an efficient route to realizing highly tunable, strong, thermally robust quantum correlations in COM systems, which is promising for the protection of fragile quantum resources.

</details>


### [117] [Quantum Metropolis-Hastings via Penalised Qubitized Walks: Spectral Filtering and Circuit Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15179)
*Miguel Carrasco-Arango,Rosa M. Badia,Artur Garcia-Saez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 量子Metropolis-Hastings算法的电路级实现与模拟研究，展示了在容错量子计算时代实现量子加速的可行性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Metropolis-Hastings算法是MCMC方法的基石，广泛应用于计算物理、贝叶斯推断和机器学习。量子版本通过量子行走承诺加速混合，但实际实现仍面临挑战，需要构建可实际实现的电路级实现方案。

Method: 基于Claudon等人提出的框架，构建并模拟了量子Metropolis-Hastings算法的显式电路级实现。提出了完整的量子工作流程来准备平稳分布，包括对算法进行多项修改以适应现实量子电路模型。

Result: 结果表明这些修改对于恢复正确的平稳行为至关重要。研究既突出了量子Metropolis-Hastings算法的潜力，也揭示了其当前局限性，预计在容错量子计算时代将变得实际相关。

Conclusion: 量子Metropolis-Hastings算法在电路级实现是可行的，但需要特定的修改来适应量子硬件限制。虽然当前仍有局限性，但在容错量子计算时代有望实现实际应用价值。

Abstract: The Metropolis-Hastings algorithm is a cornerstone of Markov Chain Monte Carlo methods, underpinning a wide range of applications in computational physics, Bayesian inference, and machine learning. Quantum variants of Metropolis-Hastings promise accelerated mixing through quantum walks, but their practical realisation remains challenging. In this work, we construct and simulate an explicit circuit level implementation of a quantum Metropolis-Hastings algorithm based on the framework introduced by Claudon \emph{et al.} (arXiv:2506.11576). We present the full quantum workflow required to prepare a stationary distribution, including a number of modifications required to make the algorithm implementable in a realistic quantum circuit model. Our results demonstrate that these modifications are essential to recover the correct stationary behaviour and highlight both the potential and current limitations of quantum Metropolis-Hastings algorithms, which are expected to become practically relevant in the fault tolerant quantum computing regime.

</details>


### [118] [Variational quantum state preparation within an entangle-rotate circuit framework for quantum-enhanced metrology in noisy systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15209)
*Juan C. Zuñiga Castro,Jeffrey Larson,Matt Menickelly,Sri Hari Krishna Narayanan,Yicheng Zhang,Michael A. Perlin,Robert J. Lewis-Swan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于变分量子电路的量子态生成方法，用于在噪声环境下优化量子费舍尔信息以提升精密测量性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在噪声的两能级系统中，如何生成适用于精密测量的量子态是一个关键挑战。传统方法在噪声环境下性能受限，需要开发能够在实际噪声条件下扩展可访问态空间的新方法。

Method: 采用受twist-and-turn方案启发的变分量子电路架构，包含n个纠缠层，每层由纠缠门和全局旋转组成。通过优化电路参数最大化输出态的量子费舍尔信息，考虑了不同退相干率和相互作用哈密顿量。该方法适用于从全连接到最近邻相互作用的幂律相互作用系统。

Result: 随着电路层深度增加，即使在显著噪声率下，量子费舍尔信息仍有显著提升。纠缠-旋转架构能够在实际噪声条件下扩展可访问态空间。该方法可扩展到超过8个量子比特的系统规模。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个通用且高效的框架，用于生成量子增强的传感态，为噪声环境下的精密测量应用提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: We investigate the generation of quantum states for precision metrology in noisy two-level systems. These states are obtained by optimizing a variational quantum circuit to maximize the quantum Fisher information (QFI) of the output state for a given decoherence rate and interaction Hamiltonian. The circuit architecture, inspired by twist-and-turn schemes, features a sequence of $n$ entangling layers, each consisting of entangling gates followed by a global rotation. We observe notable improvements in the QFI as the circuit layer depth increases, even for appreciable noise rates, demonstrating that our entangle-rotate architecture expands the accessible state space under realistic noise conditions. Our approach thus provides a general and efficient framework for generating quantum-enhanced sensing states. Our analysis extends to systems of power-law interactions spanning from all-to-all to nearest-neighbor interactions. We also analyze the capabilities of our circuit to prepare states for system sizes greater than $8$ qubits.

</details>


### [119] [Optimal algorithmic complexity of inference in quantum kernel methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15214)
*Elies Gil-fuster,Seongwook Shin,Sofiene Jerbi,Jens Eisert,Maximilian J. Kramer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 量子核方法推理的查询复杂度优化：通过单可观测量编码和量子振幅估计，将复杂度从O(N∥α∥₂²/ε²)降至O(∥α∥₁/ε)，消除了对N的依赖并实现二次改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子核方法在监督学习中具有量子优势潜力，但推理阶段存在瓶颈：评估新数据需要估计N个核值的加权和，标准方法的查询复杂度为O(N∥α∥₂²/ε²)，限制了实际应用。

Method: 系统分析两个改进维度：(1) 核值估计方法（采样vs量子振幅估计）(2) 和近似方式（逐项vs单可观测量）。将完整推理和编码为单可观测量期望值，应用量子振幅估计，实现查询最优算法。

Result: 查询最优组合达到O(∥α∥₁/ε)复杂度，消除了N的依赖，在∥α∥₁和ε上均实现二次改进。证明匹配下界Ω(∥α∥₁/ε)，确立查询最优性。分析门成本转换，发现查询最优策略在门复杂度上并非总是最优。

Conclusion: 提供了查询最优算法和根据硬件能力选择的实用最优策略，以及完整的中间方法图谱。所有算法仅需振幅估计作为子程序，适合早期容错实现，为量子核方法推理提供了系统优化框架。

Abstract: Quantum kernel methods are among the leading candidates for achieving quantum advantage in supervised learning. A key bottleneck is the cost of inference: evaluating a trained model on new data requires estimating a weighted sum $\sum_{i=1}^N α_i k(x,x_i)$ of $N$ kernel values to additive precision $\varepsilon$, where $α$ is the vector of trained coefficients. The standard approach estimates each term independently via sampling, yielding a query complexity of $O(N\lVertα\rVert_2^2/\varepsilon^2)$. In this work, we identify two independent axes for improvement: (1) How individual kernel values are estimated (sampling versus quantum amplitude estimation), and (2) how the sum is approximated (term-by-term versus via a single observable), and systematically analyze all combinations thereof. The query-optimal combination, encoding the full inference sum as the expectation value of a single observable and applying quantum amplitude estimation, achieves a query complexity of $O(\lVertα\rVert_1/\varepsilon)$, removing the dependence on $N$ from the query count and yielding a quadratic improvement in both $\lVertα\rVert_1$ and $\varepsilon$. We prove a matching lower bound of $Ω(\lVertα\rVert_1/\varepsilon)$, establishing query-optimality of our approach up to logarithmic factors. Beyond query complexity, we also analyze how these improvements translate into gate costs and show that the query-optimal strategy is not always optimal in practice from the perspective of gate complexity. Our results provide both a query-optimal algorithm and a practically optimal choice of strategy depending on hardware capabilities, along with a complete landscape of intermediate methods to guide practitioners. All algorithms require only amplitude estimation as a subroutine and are thus natural candidates for early-fault-tolerant implementations.

</details>


### [120] [Universal quantum state purification with energy-preserving operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15228)
*Xing-Chen Guo,Benchi Zhao,Xin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 建立了在能量守恒约束下的通用量子态纯化框架，针对退极化噪声，推导了通用能量保持纯化不存在的充要条件，并给出了可行时的最优协议和性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子态纯化协议通常假设无约束操作，忽略了实际量子设备固有的能量限制，需要建立能量守恒约束下的纯化理论框架

Method: 建立了通用能量守恒态纯化框架，推导了通用能量保持纯化不存在的充要条件，确定可行时的最优性能和协议，并展示如何仅使用能量保持操作系统实现

Result: 数值结果证实了所提方案的有效性，该框架将标准纯化作为特例恢复，并自然扩展到外部能量资源辅助的场景

Conclusion: 这些结果确定了态蒸馏的基本物理极限，为量子误差缓解提供了能量高效路径

Abstract: Quantum state purification, which operates not by identifying and correcting specific errors but by repeatedly projecting multiple noisy copies onto special subspaces, provides a syndrome-free alternative to quantum error correction. Existing purification protocols, however, generally assume unconstrained operations and thus overlook the energetic restrictions inherent in realistic quantum devices. Here, we establish a general framework for universal state purification under energy-conservation constraints for depolarizing noise. We derive a necessary and sufficient condition for the nonexistence of universal energy-preserving purification and, whenever such purification is feasible, analytically determine the optimal performance and the corresponding protocols. We further show how the optimal protocols can be systematically implemented using only energy-preserving operations. Numerical results confirm the effectiveness of the proposed scheme. Our framework recovers the standard purification setting as a special case and naturally extends to scenarios assisted by external energy resources. These results identify fundamental physical limits on state distillation and provide an energy-efficient route to quantum error mitigation.

</details>


### [121] [IQP circuits for 2-Forrelation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15248)
*Quentin Buzet,André Chailloux*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明2-Forrelation问题可以用IQP电路（所有门可交换的受限量子计算模型）解决，仅需两个IQP电路和两次量子查询，对于有符号变体甚至只需一个IQP电路。这回答了关于可交换量子计算能力的开放问题，并强化了BPP^IQP与PH之间的oracle分离结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2-Forrelation问题在经典与量子查询复杂度之间提供了最优分离，也是分离BQP和PH的关键问题。研究解决该问题所需的最小量子资源具有重要意义，特别是探索受限量子计算模型如IQP（瞬时量子多项式时间）电路的能力。

Method: 使用IQP电路解决2-Forrelation问题，其中IQP电路的所有门都是可交换的。对于标准2-Forrelation，采用两个IQP电路配合两次量子查询和高效经典处理；对于有符号变体，仅需一个IQP电路和一次查询。关键创新是利用二次函数Q(x)=∑_{i<j}x_ix_j的代数恒等式在IQP电路中提取内积相位。

Result: 成功证明2-Forrelation问题可以用IQP电路解决，仅需两个IQP电路和两次量子查询。对于有符号变体，仅需一个IQP电路和一次查询。这回答了Girish关于可交换量子计算能力的开放问题，并证明了(BPP^IQP)^O ⊈ PH^O相对于oracle O，强化了Raz和Tal的结果。同时证明了IQP电路的傅里叶增长界。

Conclusion: IQP电路能够解决经典困难决策问题，为通过IQP电路展示量子优势提供了新途径，避免了采样任务相关的验证困难。该工作揭示了可交换量子计算模型的计算能力，并为量子优势实验提供了理论支持。

Abstract: The 2-Forrelation problem provides an optimal separation between classical and quantum query complexity and is also the problem used for separating $\mathsf{BQP}$ and $\mathsf{PH}$ relative to an oracle. A natural question is therefore to ask what are the minimal quantum resources needed to solve this problem. We show that 2-Forrelation can be solved using Instantaneous Quantum Polynomial-time ($\mathsf{IQP}$) circuits, a restricted model of quantum computation in which all gates commute. Concretely, two $\mathsf{IQP}$ circuits with two quantum queries and efficient classical processing suffice. For the signed variant of 2-Forrelation, even a single $\mathsf{IQP}$ circuit and query suffices. This answers a recent open question of Girish (arXiv:2510.06385) on the power of commuting quantum computations. We use this to show that $(\mathsf{BPP}^{\mathsf{IQP}})^O \not\subseteq \mathsf{PH}^O$ relative to an oracle $O$, strengthening the result of Raz and Tal (STOC 2019). Our results show that $\mathsf{IQP}$ circuits can be used for classically hard decision problems, thus providing a new route for showing quantum advantage with $\mathsf{IQP}$ circuits, avoiding the verification difficulties associated with sampling tasks. We also prove Fourier growth bounds for $\mathsf{IQP}$ circuits in terms of the size of their accepting set. The key ingredient is an algebraic identity of the quadratic function $Q(x) = \sum_{i < j} x_ix_j$ that allows extracting inner-product phases within an $\mathsf{IQP}$ circuit.

</details>


### [122] [Cloning is as Hard as Learning for Stabilizer States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15269)
*Nikhil Bansal,Matthias C. Caro,Gaurav Mahajan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文研究了量子稳定子态的学习与克隆复杂度关系，证明了对于n量子比特稳定子态，最优克隆样本复杂度为Θ(n)，表明克隆与学习同样困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子理论的基础之一是无法克隆非正交态。现代量子学习理论常考虑结构化态类，并利用这种结构开发优于通用态层析的学习算法。这引出一个问题：对于这类结构化态类，学习与克隆的样本复杂度有何关系？

Method: 使用最近提出的阿贝尔态隐藏子群框架中的表示论工具，以及新引入的结构化随机纯化信道，将稳定子态克隆与经典学习理论中最近引入的样本放大问题变体联系起来。通过证明具有底层线性结构的分布类的样本放大下界，获得克隆下界。

Result: 对于n量子比特稳定子态，最优克隆样本复杂度为Θ(n)。这表明即使对于这种结构化态类，克隆与学习同样困难。

Conclusion: 结果为不可克隆定理提供了更细粒度的视角，建立了从基础理论到量子学习理论和量子密码学的连接。稳定子态克隆的困难性与学习相当，即使对于结构化态类也是如此。

Abstract: The impossibility of simultaneously cloning non-orthogonal states lies at the foundations of quantum theory. Even when allowing for approximation errors, cloning an arbitrary unknown pure state requires as many initial copies as needed to fully learn the state. Rather than arbitrary unknown states, modern quantum learning theory often considers structured classes of states and exploits such structure to develop learning algorithms that outperform general-state tomography. This raises the question: How do the sample complexities of learning and cloning relate for such structured classes? We answer this question for an important class of states. Namely, for $n$-qubit stabilizer states, we show that the optimal sample complexity of cloning is $Θ(n)$. Thus, also for this structured class of states, cloning is as hard as learning. To prove these results, we use representation-theoretic tools in the recently proposed Abelian State Hidden Subgroup framework and a new structured version of the recently introduced random purification channel to relate stabilizer state cloning to a variant of the sample amplification problem for probability distributions that was recently introduced in classical learning theory. This allows us to obtain our cloning lower bounds by proving new sample amplification lower bounds for classes of distributions with an underlying linear structure. Our results provide a more fine-grained perspective on No-Cloning theorems, opening up connections from foundations to quantum learning theory and quantum cryptography.

</details>


### [123] [Generation of Schrödinger cat-like states via degenerate dual pump spontaneous four-wave mixing in a $χ^{(3)}$ microring resonator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15275)
*Ranjit Singh,Alexander E. Teretenkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文理论研究了在χ³微环谐振腔中通过简并双泵浦自发四波混频产生非高斯量子态（特别是薛定谔猫态）的方法，通过精确解耦SPM和XPM项，将完整非线性哈密顿量简化为有效三模相互作用，并考虑腔损耗的完整量子力学方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在χ³微环谐振腔中产生非高斯量子态（特别是薛定谔猫态）的理论方法，克服传统半经典或参量近似方法的局限性，通过包含量子泵浦耗尽的完整量子力学方法，探索在耗散条件下产生和操控猫态的可能性。

Method: 引入精确解耦自相位调制和交叉相位调制项的单变换，将完整非线性哈密顿量简化为有效三模相互作用；使用Lindblad主方程研究动力学（解耦和完整哈密顿量），考虑腔损耗；计算维格纳函数、光子数分布、正交方差、法诺因子、施密特数和保真度来表征生成态。

Result: 在无耗散情况下，信号模式显示清晰的非高斯特征，具有结构化维格纳函数和偶数主导的光子数分布（类似偶相干态）；在耗散情况下（γ_j=0.2），干涉条纹变弱，奇数光子数出现，但与理想态的保真度仍高（>0.9），表明鲁棒性；泵浦模式保持高斯特性，两模式均显示超泊松统计和纠缠（>2）。

Conclusion: 简并双泵浦自发四波混频在微环谐振腔中是产生和控制耗散条件下猫态的有前景平台，完整量子力学方法能够明确包含量子泵浦耗尽，从而观察非高斯特征，即使在耗散条件下也能保持高保真度。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate the generation of non-Gaussian quantum states, specifically Schrödinger cat-like states (SCLSs), via degenerate dual-pump spontaneous four-wave mixing in a $χ^{(3)}$-based microring resonator. By introducing a unitary transformation that exactly decouples the self-phase modulation (SPM) and cross-phase modulation (XPM) terms, we reduce the full nonlinear Hamiltonian to an effective three-mode interaction. The resulting dynamics (decoupled and full Hamiltonians) are studied using the Lindblad master equation, accounting for cavity losses. Unlike semiclassical or parametric approximations, our full quantum mechanical approach explicitly includes quantum pump depletion, which enables the emergence and observation of non-Gaussian features. We compute the Wigner function, photon number distributions, quadrature variances, Fano factor, Schmidt number, and fidelity to characterize the generated states. For the non-dissipative case, we find that the signal mode $\hat{b}_3$ or $\hat{a}_3$ exhibits clear non-Gaussian features with a structured Wigner function and even-dominated photon number distribution, characteristic of an even coherent state. In the presence of dissipation ($γ_j = 0.2$), the interference fringes become faint, odd photon numbers appear, and the fidelity with the ideal state remains high ($>0.9$), indicating robustness. The pump mode $\hat{b}_1$ or $\hat{a}_1$ remains Gaussian, while both modes display super-Poissonian statistics and entanglement ($>2$). Our results demonstrate that degenerate dual-pump spontaneous four-wave mixing in microring resonators is a promising platform for generating and controlling cat-like states under dissipative conditions.

</details>


### [124] [Super-Constant Weight Dicke States in Constant Depth Without Fanout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15298)
*Lucas Gretta,Meghal Gupta,Malvika Raj Joshi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了在QAC⁰电路中构建Dicke态和对称态的新方法，使用多量子比特Toffoli门和单量子比特酉操作，实现了对超常数权重Dicke态的首次QAC⁰构造，并建立了FANOUT门需求与权重之间的紧致对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dicke态作为重要的纠缠资源在NISQ时代具有实际应用价值，特别是在解码量子干涉仪中扮演核心角色。然而，先前构建超常数权重Dicke态需要FANOUT_n门，而QAC⁰电路只能实现polylog(n)规模的FANOUT。因此需要开发更高效的构造方法。

Method: 使用多量子比特Toffoli门和单量子比特酉操作，给出显式的常数深度电路来制备所有k≤polylog(n)的n量子比特Dicke态。证明权重k的Dicke态仅需要FANOUT_min(k,n-k)而非FANOUT_n。进一步扩展到任意权重不超过k的Dicke态叠加态。

Result: 首次实现了超常数权重Dicke态的QAC⁰构造，建立了紧致特征：对于k≤n/2，权重k的Dicke态可在QAC⁰中制备当且仅当FANOUT_k∈QAC⁰。获得了任意对称态的O(1)深度酉构造，适用于支持FANOUT_n的量子硬件架构。

Conclusion: 该工作为Dicke态和对称态提供了高效的常数深度构造方案，建立了FANOUT门需求与Dicke态权重之间的精确对应关系，为NISQ时代量子算法实现提供了重要工具，特别适用于具有全局纠缠操作的离子阱系统。

Abstract: An $n$-qubit Dicke state of weight $k$, is the uniform superposition over all $n$-bit strings of Hamming weight $k$. Dicke states are an entanglement resource with important practical applications in the NISQ era and, for instance, play a central role in Decoded Quantum Interferometry (DQI). Furthermore, any symmetric state can be expressed as a superposition of Dicke states.
  First, we give explicit constant-depth circuits that prepare $n$-qubit Dicke states for all $k \leq \text{polylog}(n)$, using only multi-qubit Toffoli gates and single-qubit unitaries. This gives the first $\text{QAC}^0$ construction of super-constant weight Dicke states. Previous constant-depth constructions for any super-constant $k$ required the FANOUT$_n$ gate, while $\text{QAC}^0$ is only known to implement FANOUT$_k$ for $k$ up to $\text{polylog}(n)$. Moreover, we show that any weight-$k$ Dicke state can be constructed with access to FANOUT$_{\min(k,n-k)}$, rather than FANOUT$_n$. Combined with recent hardness results, this yields a tight characterization: for $k \leq n/2$, weight-$k$ Dicke states can be prepared in $\text{QAC}^0$ if and only if FANOUT$_k \in \text{QAC}^0$.
  We further extend our techniques to show that, in fact, \emph{any} superposition of $n$-qubit Dicke states of weight at most $k$ can be prepared in $\text{QAC}^0$ with access to FANOUT$_k$. Taking $k = n$, we obtain the first $O(1)$-depth unitary construction for arbitrary symmetric states. In particular, any symmetric state can be prepared in constant depth on quantum hardware architectures that support FANOUT$_n$, such as trapped ions with native global entangling operations.

</details>


### [125] [Heuristic Search for Minimum-Distance Upper-Bound Witnesses in Quantum APM-LDPC Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15307)
*Kenta Kasai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了基于仿射置换矩阵构造的Calderbank-Shor-Steane量子LDPC码族的最小距离的认证上界，通过构建低权重非稳定子逻辑代表元来获得有效上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是获得量子LDPC码最小距离的严格上界，而非尝试证明一般下界。由于量子码距离计算困难，作者聚焦于构建低权重逻辑操作符来提供有效的距离上界。

Method: 开发了统一的框架来构建认证上界的见证元，包括：潜在行关系、受限提升子空间（块压缩、选择纤维、CRT条纹构造）、循环-8基本陷阱集结构、解码器失败残差。所有方法都只使用搜索生成候选，通过显式的核和行空间排除测试后才报告边界。

Result: 将方法应用于代表性APM-LDPC码，锐化了先前报告的上界，并在探索的参数范围内提供了具体的认证值。对于潜在部分，还识别了块压缩准则，在该准则下认证变得精确。

Conclusion: 该研究为量子LDPC码距离分析提供了系统化的认证上界框架，通过多种构造方法获得比先前更精确的上界，为量子纠错码设计提供了重要的理论工具。

Abstract: This paper investigates certified upper bounds on the minimum distance of an explicit family of Calderbank-Shor-Steane quantum LDPC codes constructed from affine permutation matrices. All codes considered here have active Tanner graphs of girth eight. Rather than attempting to prove a general lower bound for the full code distance, we focus on constructing low-weight non-stabilizer logical representatives, which yield valid upper bounds once they are verified to lie in the opposite parity-check kernel and outside the stabilizer row space. We develop a unified framework for such witnesses arising from latent row relations, restricted-lift subspaces including block-compressed, selected-fiber, and CRT-stripe constructions, cycle- 8 elementary trapping-set structures, and decoder-failure residuals. In every case, search is used only to generate candidates; the reported bounds begin only after explicit kernel and row-space exclusion tests have been passed. For the latent part, we also identify a block-compression criterion under which the certification becomes exact. Applying these methods to representative APM-LDPC codes sharpens previously reported upper bounds and provides concrete certified values across the explored parameter range.

</details>
